____________ test_unresolved_parent_known_supports_precise_removal ____________

ledger = <agent.security.requirements.RequirementLedger object at 0x000002292936B650>

    def test_unresolved_parent_known_supports_precise_removal(ledger):
        b_path = "D:/proj/B.py"
        write_b = limit(("write",), (obj("path", b_path),), "不要写 B")
        delete_b = limit(("delete",), (obj("path", b_path),), "不要删 B")
        parent = persist(
            ledger,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
            limits=(write_b, delete_b),
        )
        applied = amend(
            ledger,
            parent,
            message="B 可以写了，仍然不要删除。",
            removed=(limit(("write",), (obj("path", b_path),)),),
        )
        child = applied.requirement
        assert child is not None
        # 父未恢复完整，子仍 unresolved；但 known 必须继承并精确移除 write
        assert child.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED
>       assert child.structured_limits == (delete_b,)

ledger = <agent.security.requirements.RequirementLedger object at 0x000002292A3DB410>

    def test_remove_part_of_all_operations_entry(ledger):
        b_path = "D:/proj/B.py"
        parent = persist(
            ledger,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED,
            limits=(limit((), (obj("path", b_path),), "B 上所有操作都禁止"),),
        )
        applied = amend(
            ledger,
            parent,
            message="B 可以写，但其他操作仍不允许。",
            removed=(limit(("write",), (obj("path", b_path),)),),
        )
        child = applied.requirement
        assert child is not None
        # 无法表达"除 write 外的所有操作"：保守保留原限制并标 unresolved，不猜测
        assert child.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED
>       assert child.structured_limits == (
            limit((), (obj("path", b_path),), "B 上所有操作都禁止"),
        )

    def test_ambiguous_removal_preserves_known_limits_and_marks_unresolved(ledger):

            ledger,
            parent,

            removed=(limit(("write",), (obj("path", "D:/proj/C.py"),)),),
        )
        child = applied.requirement
        assert child is not None

_______________ test_removed_limits_can_target_read_and_program _______________

ledger = <agent.security.requirements.RequirementLedger object at 0x000002292A5A52D0>

    def test_removed_limits_can_target_read_and_program(ledger):
        parent = persist(
            ledger,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED,
            limits=(
                limit(("read",), (obj("path", "D:/secret.txt"),), "不要读取"),
                limit(("execute",), (obj("program", "python"),), "不要运行 python"),
            ),
        )
        applied = amend(
            ledger,
            parent,
            message="python 可以运行了，读取限制保留。",
            removed=(limit(("execute",), (obj("program", "python"),)),),
        )
        child = applied.requirement
        assert child is not None
        assert child.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED
>       assert child.structured_limits == (
            limit(("read",), (obj("path", "D:/secret.txt"),), "不要读取"),
        )

    def test_invalid_resolution_limits_preserve_parent_known(ledger):

            ledger, state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED, limits=(b_write,)
        )

            relation=RequirementRelation.AMEND,
            target_requirement_id=parent.requirement_id,
            target_version=int(parent.version),
            corrections=("假装输出非法限制",),
            limits=({"operations": ["keyword_allow"], "objects": []},),
            reason="非法限制测试",
            model="fake-r9h4",
            prompt_version="r9h4-test",
        )
        applied = ledger.apply_resolution(
            outcome, message_id="m_bad", turn_id="t_bad", message="非法限制"
        )
        child = applied.requirement
        assert child is not None
        assert child.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED

    def test_set_structured_limits_failure_does_not_clear_known(ledger):

        requirement = persist(

        )

    def test_unresolved_keeps_known_entries_but_not_complete(conn):
        ledger = RequirementLedger(conn)
        requirement = ledger.record_user_message(text="无法可靠恢复", message_id="m1", turn_id="t1")
        entry = _limit("/work/report.md")
        ledger.set_structured_limits(
            requirement.requirement_id,
            [entry],
            source="recovery:model-timeout",
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
        )
        stored = ledger.by_id(requirement.requirement_id)
        assert stored is not None
        assert stored.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED

    def test_amend_merge_add_and_explicit_remove(conn):
        ledger = RequirementLedger(conn)
        parent = ledger.record_user_message(text="整理报告", message_id="m1", turn_id="t1")
        entry_a = _limit("/work/report.md")
        ledger.set_structured_limits(parent.requirement_id, [entry_a], source="test")
    
        entry_b = _limit("/work/other.md", operations=("write", "delete"), reason="新增限制")
        amended = ledger.apply_resolution(
            _resolution(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND.value,
                target=parent.requirement_id,
                version=parent.version,
                limits=[entry_b],
            ),
            message_id="m2",
            turn_id="t2",
            message="另外不要改 /work/other.md 的删除？",
        )
        assert amended.relation == "amend"
        child = amended.requirement
        assert child is not None
        assert amended.limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED

____________ test_amend_from_legacy_parent_keeps_unresolved_state _____________

conn = <sqlite3.Connection object at 0x000002292A4AAA70>

    def test_amend_from_legacy_parent_keeps_unresolved_state(conn):
        ledger = RequirementLedger(conn)
        parent = ledger.record_user_message(text="历史要求", message_id="m1", turn_id="t1")
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE user_requirements SET structured_limits = NULL,"
            " structured_limits_state = 'legacy', structured_limits_source = NULL"
            " WHERE id = ?",
            (parent.requirement_id,),
        )
        conn.commit()
        known = _limit("/work/new.md")
        amended = ledger.apply_resolution(
            _resolution(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND.value,
                target=parent.requirement_id,
                version=parent.version,
                limits=[known],
            ),
            message_id="m2",
            turn_id="t2",
            message="补充：别动 new.md",
        )
        child = amended.requirement
        assert child is not None
        assert child.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_LEGACY
        assert requirement_limits.limits_from_requirement(child) is None, "父未解决不得声称完整"
>       assert child.structured_limits == (known,), "已知新增可保存，但状态仍未解决"

ctx = <agent.services.app.AppContext object at 0x000002294AD86210>
tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/zxy/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-zxy/pytest-4434/test_legacy_root_plus_title_on0')

    async def test_legacy_root_plus_title_only_keeps_b_restriction(ctx, tmp_path):
        b_path = tmp_path / "proj" / "B.py"
        a_path = tmp_path / "proj" / "A.py"
        root = seed_legacy(ctx, "修复项目，但不要修改 %s。" % b_path)
        assert root.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_LEGACY
    
        resolver = FakeChainResolver(
            outcome=make_outcome(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND,
                target=root,
                target_version=int(root.version),
                corrections=("把标题改成 X",),
            )
        )
        message = "先把标题改成 X。"
        turn_ctx, observed = await run_turn(ctx, message=message, resolver=resolver)
        binding = observed["binding"]
        assert isinstance(binding, RequirementBinding)
        child = ctx.requirements.by_id(binding.requirement_id)
        assert child is not None
        assert int(child.version) == 2
        # 旧 app 分支会把 legacy 子要求标 unresolved；核心断言：未解决、不得当无限制。
        assert child.structured_limits_state in (
            requirement_limits.STATE_LEGACY,
            requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
        )
    
        context = ctx.requirements.recovery_context(child.requirement_id)
        assert isinstance(context, RequirementRecoveryContext)
        assert context.complete is True, context.reason
        assert [node.requirement_id for node in context.chain] == [
            root.requirement_id,
            child.requirement_id,
        ]
        assert [node.text for node in context.chain] == [root.text, message]
        assert [node.version for node in context.chain] == [1, 2]
        assert context.chain[1].parent_id == root.requirement_id
        assert len(context.chain_digest) == 64
    
        resolver.limits = (
            {
                "operations": ["write"],
                "objects": [{"kind": "path", "value": str(b_path)}],
                "reason": "用户要求不要修改 B",
            },
        )
        limits, saved, used_context = await recover(ctx, resolver, child.requirement_id)
        assert saved.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED

ctx = <agent.services.app.AppContext object at 0x000002294ABD2E90>
tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/zxy/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-zxy/pytest-4434/test_multiple_amendments_keep_0')

    async def test_multiple_amendments_keep_original_restriction(ctx, tmp_path):
        b_path = tmp_path / "proj" / "B.py"
        root = seed_legacy(ctx, "修复项目，但不要修改 %s。" % b_path)
    
        resolver1 = FakeChainResolver(
            outcome=make_outcome(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND,
                target=root,
                target_version=int(root.version),
                corrections=("把标题改成 X",),
            )
        )
        _, observed1 = await run_turn(
            ctx, message="先把标题改成 X。", resolver=resolver1
        )
        binding1 = observed1["binding"]
        assert isinstance(binding1, RequirementBinding)
        first_child = ctx.requirements.by_id(binding1.requirement_id)
        assert first_child is not None and int(first_child.version) == 2
    
        resolver2 = FakeChainResolver(
            outcome=make_outcome(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND,
                target=first_child,
                target_version=int(first_child.version),
                corrections=("再加一句结论",),
            )
        )
        _, observed2 = await run_turn(
            ctx, message="再加一句结论。", resolver=resolver2
        )
        binding2 = observed2["binding"]
        assert isinstance(binding2, RequirementBinding)
        latest = ctx.requirements.by_id(binding2.requirement_id)
        assert latest is not None and int(latest.version) == 3
        assert latest.structured_limits_state in (
            requirement_limits.STATE_LEGACY,
            requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
        )
    
        context = ctx.requirements.recovery_context(latest.requirement_id)
        assert context.complete is True, context.reason
        assert [node.text for node in context.chain] == [
            root.text,
            "先把标题改成 X。",
            "再加一句结论。",
        ]
        assert [node.version for node in context.chain] == [1, 2, 3]
    
        resolver2.limits = (
            {
                "operations": ["write"],
                "objects": [{"kind": "path", "value": str(b_path)}],
                "reason": "原要求明确不要修改 B",
            },
        )
        limits, saved, _ = await recover(ctx, resolver2, latest.requirement_id)
        assert saved.structured_limits_state == requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED

ctx = <agent.services.app.AppContext object at 0x000002294BA96110>
tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/zxy/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-zxy/pytest-4434/test_conditional_write_rejects0')

    async def test_conditional_write_rejects_stale_version_and_preserves_known_limits(ctx, tmp_path):
        b_path = tmp_path / "proj" / "B.py"
        root = seed_legacy(ctx, "不要修改 %s。" % b_path)
        child = ctx.requirements.add_correction(root.requirement_id, "把标题改成 X。")
        known = (
            {
                "operations": ["write"],
                "objects": [{"kind": "path", "value": str(b_path)}],
                "reason": "旧恢复结果",
            },
        )
        child = ctx.requirements.set_structured_limits(
            child.requirement_id,
            known,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
            source="prior_recovery",
        )
        context_before = ctx.requirements.recovery_context(child.requirement_id)
        assert context_before.complete is True
    
        # 真实主流程：再加一次修正 → 新版本
        resolver = FakeChainResolver(
            outcome=make_outcome(
                RequirementRelation.AMEND,
                target=child,
                target_version=int(child.version),
                corrections=("再改一次标题",),
            )
        )
        _, observed = await run_turn(ctx, message="再改一次标题。", resolver=resolver)
        binding = observed["binding"]
        assert isinstance(binding, RequirementBinding)
        assert int(binding.version) == 3
    
        # 用旧版本/旧链摘要写回 → 必须拒绝
        with pytest.raises(RequirementVersionMismatch):
            ctx.requirements.set_structured_limits(
                child.requirement_id,
                known,
                state=requirement_limits.STATE_STRUCTURED,
                source="stale_recovery",
                expected_version=int(child.version),
                expected_status="active",
                expected_chain_digest=context_before.chain_digest,
            )
        # 旧行（已被 supersede）与 new 行都不得被旧结果覆盖
        old_row = ctx.requirements.by_id(child.requirement_id)
        assert old_row is not None
        assert old_row.status == "superseded"

ctx = <agent.services.app.AppContext object at 0x000002294B758350>

    async def test_recovery_failure_keeps_known_limits_and_source(ctx):
        root = seed_legacy(ctx, "不要修改 B。")
        child = ctx.requirements.add_correction(root.requirement_id, "把标题改成 X。")
        known = (
            {
                "operations": ["write"],
                "objects": [{"kind": "path", "value": "B"}],
                "reason": "之前已知的限制",
            },
        )
        ctx.requirements.set_structured_limits(
            child.requirement_id,
            known,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
            source="prior_recovery",
        )
        resolver = FakeChainResolver(error=RequirementResolutionUnavailable("模型超时"))
        with pytest.raises(RequirementResolutionUnavailable):
            await recover(ctx, resolver, child.requirement_id)
    
        # 契约 §3.3-6：失败时保留已有 known limits，不得用空数组覆盖
        row = ctx.requirements.by_id(child.requirement_id)
        assert row is not None
        ctx.requirements.set_structured_limits(
            child.requirement_id,
            row.structured_limits,
            state=requirement_limits.STATE_UNRESOLVED,
            source="recovery_failed:RequirementResolutionUnavailable",
        )
        failed = ctx.requirements.by_id(child.requirement_id)
        assert failed is not None
